```python
def terminate_preflight_group(process):
    term_end = time.monotonic() + 2
    kill_end = term_end + 2
    try:
        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        process.wait(timeout=max(0, term_end - time.monotonic()))
        return True
    while time.monotonic() < term_end:
        process.poll()
        try:
            os.killpg(process.pid, 0)
        except ProcessLookupError:
            process.wait(timeout=max(0, term_end - time.monotonic()))
            return True
        time.sleep(min(0.02, max(0, term_end - time.monotonic())))
    try:
        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        pass
    while time.monotonic() < kill_end:
        process.poll()
        try:
            os.killpg(process.pid, 0)
        except ProcessLookupError:
            process.wait(timeout=max(0, kill_end - time.monotonic()))
            return True
        time.sleep(min(0.02, max(0, kill_end - time.monotonic())))
    return False

def run_cached_preflight(argv, environment):
    global owner_work_bytes, frame, runner_attempted
    assert 532480 <= 67108864 - owner_work_bytes
    owner_work_bytes += 532480
    assert owner_work_bytes == boot['parent_work']
    buffers = {'stdout': bytearray(), 'stderr': bytearray()}
    process, selector, gone = None, None, False
    causes = []
    frame = None
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 30
    try:
        runner_attempted = True
        process = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=B, env=environment, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, start_new_session=True)
        assert time.monotonic() < deadline
        selector = selectors.DefaultSelector()
        for name, stream in (('stdout', process.stdout), ('stderr', process.stderr)):
            os.set_blocking(stream.fileno(), False)
            selector.register(stream, selectors.EVENT_READ, name)
        while selector.get_map() or process.poll() is None:
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            assert remaining > 0, 'VM supervision30second deadline'
            for key, _ in selector.select(min(0.1, remaining)):
                chunk = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 4096)
                if not chunk:
                    selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
                    key.fileobj.close()
                    continue
                assert len(chunk) <= 65536 - len(buffers[key.data])
                buffers[key.data].extend(chunk)
        process.wait(timeout=max(0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    except BaseException as error:
        causes.append('runner:' + type(error).__name__)
    finally:
        if process is None:
            gone = not runner_attempted
        else:
            try:
                gone = terminate_preflight_group(process)
            except BaseException as error:
                causes.append('group:' + type(error).__name__)
        if selector is not None:
            try:
                selector.close()
            except BaseException as error:
                causes.append('selector:' + type(error).__name__)
        if process is not None:
            for stream in (process.stdout, process.stderr):
                if stream is not None and not stream.closed:
                    try:
                        stream.close()
                    except BaseException as error:
                        causes.append('pipe:' + type(error).__name__)
    result = {
        'pid': None if process is None else process.pid,
        'exit': None if process is None else process.returncode, 'failures': causes,
        'group_gone': gone, 'output_encoding': 'hex',
        'stdout': bytes(buffers['stdout']).hex(), 'stderr': bytes(buffers['stderr']).hex(),
    }
    try:
        assert result['exit'] == 0 and not causes and gone
        stdout, stderr = bytes(buffers['stdout']), bytes(buffers['stderr'])
        prefix = b'WF_SUPPORTED_PROJECT_READINESS_V1 '
        assert stderr == b'' and len(stdout) <= 32768
        assert stdout.startswith(prefix) and stdout.endswith(b'\n') and stdout.count(b'\n') == 1
        frame = json.loads(stdout[len(prefix):-1].decode('utf-8', 'strict'),
                           object_pairs_hook=unique_json_object)
        assert type(frame) is dict and set(frame) == FRAME_KEYS
        assert type(frame['schema_version']) is int and frame['schema_version'] == 1
        assert frame['kind'] == 'supported-original-project-readiness'
        use = frame['owner']
        assert type(use) is dict and set(use) == {'parent_work', 'future_reserve', 'child_allowance', 'used'}
        assert all(type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= 67108864 for value in use.values())
        assert (use['parent_work'], use['future_reserve'], use['child_allowance']) == (
            boot['parent_work'], future_reserve, child_allowance)
        assert use['used'] <= child_allowance
        core = frame['core']
        assert type(core) is dict and set(core) == CORE_KEYS
        assert (core['pid'], core['elixir_version'], core['otp_release'], core['erts_version'], core['code_root']) == (
            str(result['pid']), '1.20.4', '29', '17.0.5', str(R))
        build = core['build_info']
        assert type(build) is dict and set(build) == BUILD_INFO_KEYS
        assert all(type(value) is str and len(value.encode('utf-8')) <= 4096 for value in build.values())
        assert build['version'] == '1.20.4' and build['otp_release'] == '29'
        assert type(core['architecture']) is str and len(core['architecture'].encode('utf-8')) <= 4096
        assert core['architecture'].startswith(('aarch64-apple-darwin', 'arm64-apple-darwin'))
        assert core['os_type'] == ['unix', 'darwin']
        assert type(core['os_version']) is list and len(core['os_version']) == 3
        assert all(type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= 18446744073709551615 for value in core['os_version'])
        assert core['os_version'] == [int(value) for value in os.uname().release.split('.')]
        assert type(core['modules']) is list and len(core['modules']) == 4
        for expected, actual in zip(modules, core['modules']):
            assert type(actual) is dict and set(actual) == {'module', 'path', 'sha256', 'loaded_md5'}
            assert all(type(value) is str for value in actual.values())
            assert {key: actual[key] for key in expected} == expected
            assert len(actual['loaded_md5']) == 32 and all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in actual['loaded_md5'])
        assert core['sha256_abc'] == hashlib.sha256(b'abc').hexdigest()
        assert type(core['json_encoded']) is str and len(core['json_encoded'].encode('utf-8')) <= 1024
        for decoded in (json.loads(core['json_encoded'], object_pairs_hook=unique_json_object), core['json_decoded']):
            assert type(decoded) is dict and set(decoded) == {'empty', 'text', 'values'}
            assert type(decoded['empty']) is list and decoded['empty'] == []
            assert type(decoded['text']) is str and decoded['text'] == 'é\n"\\'
            assert type(decoded['values']) is list and len(decoded['values']) == 5
            assert [type(value) for value in decoded['values']] == [type(None), bool, bool, int, int]
            assert decoded['values'] == [None, True, False, 0, 18446744073709551615]
        assert type(core['crypto_libraries']) is list and 1 <= len(core['crypto_libraries']) <= 4
        for row in core['crypto_libraries']:
            assert type(row) is dict and set(row) == {'name', 'version', 'text'}
            assert all(type(row[key]) is str and len(row[key].encode('utf-8')) <= 256 for key in ('name', 'text'))
            assert type(row['version']) is int and 0 <= row['version'] <= 18446744073709551615
    except BaseException as error:
        frame = None
        causes.append('core-frame:' + type(error).__name__)
    return result

def unique_json_object(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError('duplicate JSON object key')
        result[key] = value
    return result

    opened = os.fstat(fd)

        getattr(carrier_info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
    carrier_buffer = bytearray()
    while len(carrier_buffer) < opened.st_size:
        count = min(65536, opened.st_size - len(carrier_buffer), 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes)
        assert count > 0
        chunk = os.read(fd, count)
        snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
        assert chunk, 'short EOF before admitted carrier size'
        carrier_buffer.extend(chunk)
    final = os.fstat(fd)
    assert tuple(getattr(final, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
        getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
except BaseException as error:
    carrier_error = error
finally:
    try:
        os.close(fd)
    except BaseException as close_error:
        if carrier_error is not None:
            raise BaseExceptionGroup('carrier read and close', [carrier_error, close_error])
        raise

    # This already-active try/finally protects exclusive ownership immediately.
    stage_info = stage.lstat()
    assert stat.S_ISDIR(stage_info.st_mode) and stat.S_IMODE(stage_info.st_mode) == 0o700
    assert stage_info.st_uid == os.getuid()
    stage_identity = (stage_info.st_dev, stage_info.st_ino)
    for relative in ('tmp', 'mix-home', 'mix-home/archives', 'build'):
        os.mkdir(stage / relative, 0o700)
    assert os.listdir(stage / 'mix-home/archives') == []
    modules = [{'module': name, 'path': str(path), 'sha256': before['files'][str(path)][9]} for name, path in MODULES.items()]
    projects = [{'app': row[0], 'module': row[1], 'file': str(B / row[2] / 'mix.exs'),
                 'sha256': before['files'][str(B / row[2] / 'mix.exs')][9]} for row in PROJECTS]
    boot = {
        'modules': modules, 'projects': projects, 'matches': before['matches'], 'census': before['census'],
        'stage': str(stage), 'uid': os.getuid(), 'parent_work': owner_work_bytes + 532480,
        'future_reserve': future_reserve, 'child_allowance': child_allowance,
    }
    boot_json = json.dumps(boot, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
    assert len(boot_json) <= 16384
    assert expression_template.count('__BOOTSTRAP_HEX__') == 1
    assert len(expression_template.encode('utf-8')) - 17 + 2 * len(boot_json) <= 32768
    expression = expression_template.replace('__BOOTSTRAP_HEX__', boot_json.hex())
    assert len(expression.encode('utf-8')) <= 32768
    argv = [
        '/bin/sh', '-c', 'ulimit -c 0 || exit; exec "$@"', 'wf-supported-project-readiness',
        str(E / 'bin/elixir'), '--no-color', '--erl',
        '+S 2:2 +SDcpu 1 +SDio 1 +A 1 -noinput -boot ' + str(R / 'releases/29/start_clean'),
        '-e', expression,
    ]
    before_encoded = json.dumps(before, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
    before_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(before_encoded).hexdigest()
    source_hashes = [hashlib.sha256(part).hexdigest() for part in bound_source_bytes]
    pre_value = {
        'kind': 'R-MIX1 assigned original-project-only preflight; full profiles remain open',
        'source_parts': source_parts, 'source_sha256': source_hashes, 'sources_v2_sha256': source_composite,
        'carrier_sha256': carrier_sha256, 'argv': argv, 'environment': environment,
        'before': before, 'before_sha256': before_sha256, 'stage': str(stage),
        'account': {'before_runner': owner_work_bytes, 'runner': 532480, 'after': after_reserve,
                    'tree': tree_reserve, 'future': future_reserve, 'child': child_allowance},
        'external_git_prerequisite': {
            'expected_head_source_literal': BASELINE_HEAD,
            'fresh_root_pre_and_post_receipts_required': True,
            'program_observed_git': False,
        },
    }
    pre_size = 1
    for fragment in json.JSONEncoder(sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).iterencode(pre_value):
        pre_size += len(fragment.encode('ascii'))
        assert pre_size + post_reserve <= 2097152
    pre_record = (json.dumps(pre_value, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')) + '\n').encode('ascii')
    assert len(pre_record) == pre_size
    log_info = LOG.lstat()
    assert stat.S_ISREG(log_info.st_mode) and log_info.st_nlink == 1 and log_info.st_uid == os.getuid()
    assert log_info.st_size + pre_size + post_reserve <= 8388608
    assert log_info.st_size <= 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes
    fd = os.open(LOG, os.O_RDWR | os.O_APPEND | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
    archive_error = None
    try:
        opened = os.fstat(fd)
        assert tuple(getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
            getattr(log_info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
        trace_identity = (opened.st_dev, opened.st_ino)
        while len(trace_prefix) < opened.st_size:
            count = min(65536, opened.st_size - len(trace_prefix), 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes)
            assert count > 0
            chunk = os.read(fd, count)
            snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
            assert chunk, 'short EOF reading preserved trace'
            trace_prefix.extend(chunk)
        stable = os.fstat(fd)
        assert tuple(getattr(stable, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
            getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
        offset = 0
        while offset < len(pre_record):
            count = os.write(fd, pre_record[offset:offset + 65536])
            assert count > 0
            offset += count
        os.fsync(fd)
        written = os.fstat(fd)
        assert (written.st_dev, written.st_ino, written.st_mode, written.st_uid, written.st_nlink) == (
            opened.st_dev, opened.st_ino, opened.st_mode, opened.st_uid, opened.st_nlink)
        assert written.st_size == len(trace_prefix) + len(pre_record)
        assert written.st_size <= 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes
        os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
        for part in (trace_prefix, pre_record):
            offset = 0
            while offset < len(part):
                count = min(65536, len(part) - offset, 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes)
                assert count > 0
                chunk = os.read(fd, count)
                snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
                assert chunk == part[offset:offset + len(chunk)] and chunk
                offset += len(chunk)
        stable = os.fstat(fd)
        assert tuple(getattr(stable, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
            getattr(written, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
    except BaseException as error:
        archive_error = error
    finally:
        try:
            os.close(fd)
        except BaseException as close_error:
            if archive_error is not None:
                raise BaseExceptionGroup('prearchive and close', [archive_error, close_error])
            raise
    if archive_error is not None:
        raise archive_error
    current = LOG.lstat()
    assert tuple(getattr(current, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
        getattr(written, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
    pre_archived = True
    result = run_cached_preflight(argv, environment)
    assert frame is not None and result['exit'] == 0 and result['group_gone'] and not result['failures']
    assert type(frame['projects']) is list and len(frame['projects']) == 5
    for expected, actual, source in zip(projects, frame['projects'], PROJECTS):
        assert type(actual) is dict and set(actual) == {
            'app', 'module', 'file', 'source_sha256', 'loaded_md5', 'build_path', 'config_path',
            'deps_path', 'lockfile', 'elixirc_paths', 'elixir', 'config_bytes'}
        assert (actual['app'], actual['module'], actual['file'], actual['source_sha256']) == (
            expected['app'], expected['module'], expected['file'], expected['sha256'])
        assert type(actual['loaded_md5']) is str and len(actual['loaded_md5']) == 32
        assert all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in actual['loaded_md5'])
        assert actual['build_path'] == build_path
        assert (actual['config_path'], actual['deps_path'], actual['lockfile']) == (
            str(B / 'config/config.exs'), str(B / 'deps'), str(B / 'mix.lock'))
        assert type(actual['elixirc_paths']) is list and actual['elixirc_paths'] == source[3]
        assert actual['elixir'] == (None if source[0] == 'ferricstore_umbrella' else '~> 1.20')
        assert type(actual['config_bytes']) is int and 0 <= actual['config_bytes'] <= 16777216
    assert type(frame['docs']) is dict and set(frame['docs']) == {'guides', 'docs'}
    assert frame['docs'] == before['matches']
    config = frame['config']
    assert type(config) is dict and set(config) == CONFIG_KEYS
    assert config['imports'] == sorted([str(B / 'config/config.exs'), str(B / 'config/test.exs')])
    assert config['runtime_imports'] == [str(B / 'config/runtime.exs')]
    for field in ('config_bytes', 'runtime_bytes', 'environment_bytes'):
        assert type(config[field]) is int and 0 <= config[field] <= 16777216
    for field in ('config_sha256', 'runtime_sha256'):
        assert type(config[field]) is str and len(config[field]) == 64
        assert all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in config[field])
    selected = config['selected']
    assert type(selected) is dict and set(selected) == SELECTED_KEYS
    assert type(selected['ports']) is list and len(selected['ports']) == 3
    assert all(type(value) is int and value == 0 for value in selected['ports'])
    assert type(selected['workers']) is list and len(selected['workers']) == 2
    assert all(value is False for value in selected['workers'])
    assert type(selected['force_build']) is list and len(selected['force_build']) == 2
    assert all(value is True for value in selected['force_build'])
    assert type(selected['nif_skip']) is list and len(selected['nif_skip']) == 2
    assert all(value is False for value in selected['nif_skip'])
    assert selected['topologies_disabled'] is True and selected['data_dir_auto_cleanup'] is True
    data_dir = selected['data_dir']
    assert type(data_dir) is str and len(data_dir.encode('utf-8')) <= 4096
    data_path = pathlib.Path(data_dir)
    assert data_path.parent == stage / 'tmp'
    data_name_prefix = 'ferricstore_test_' + str(result['pid']) + '_'
    assert data_path.name.startswith(data_name_prefix)
    suffix = data_path.name[len(data_name_prefix):]
    assert len(suffix) == 16 and all(c in alphabet for c in suffix)
    assert not os.path.lexists(data_path)
    assert type(frame['processes']) is dict and set(frame['processes']) == set(MIX_PROCESSES)
    for name, pid in frame['processes'].items():
        if name.endswith('.ListenerSupervisor'):
            assert pid is None
        else:
            assert type(pid) is str and 0 < len(pid) <= 64 and pid.startswith('<') and pid.endswith('>')
            assert all(piece.isascii() and piece.isdigit() for piece in pid[1:-1].split('.'))
            assert len(pid[1:-1].split('.')) == 3
    assert type(frame['pubsub']) is dict and set(frame['pubsub']) == {'path', 'port'}
    assert type(frame['pubsub']['port']) is int and 1 <= frame['pubsub']['port'] <= 65535
    assert frame['pubsub']['path'] == str(pub_directory / ('port_' + str(frame['pubsub']['port'])))
    lock = frame['lock']
    assert type(lock) is dict and set(lock) == LOCK_KEYS
    assert type(lock['uid']) is int and lock['uid'] == os.getuid()
    assert type(lock['port']) is int and 1 <= lock['port'] <= 65535
    assert lock['key'] == build_key and lock['path'] == str(lock_directory)
    for field in ('active', 'released'):
        assert type(lock[field]) is str and len(lock[field]) == 150
        assert all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in lock[field])
    active, released = bytes.fromhex(lock['active']), bytes.fromhex(lock['released'])
    assert active[0] in (0, 1) and released[0] == 1 - active[0]
    segments = (active[1:38], active[38:75])
    segment = segments[active[0]]
    pid_bytes = str(result['pid']).encode('ascii')
    assert 0 < len(pid_bytes) <= 32
    expected_active = lock['port'].to_bytes(4, 'big') + bytes([32 - len(pid_bytes)]) + bytes(32 - len(pid_bytes)) + pid_bytes
    expected_released = bytes(4) + bytes([32]) + bytes(32)
    assert segments == (expected_active, expected_active)
    assert (released[1:38], released[38:75])[released[0]] == expected_released
    assert (released[1:38], released[38:75])[1 - released[0]] == expected_active
    assert frame['started_live'] == ['compiler', 'elixir', 'kernel', 'logger', 'mix', 'stdlib']
    assert frame['started_after'] == ['compiler', 'elixir', 'kernel', 'logger', 'stdlib']
    assert type(frame['origins']) is list and len(frame['origins']) <= 4096
    seen_modules = set()
    for row in frame['origins']:
        assert type(row) is list and len(row) == 5
        assert all(type(value) is str and len(value.encode('utf-8')) <= 4096 for value in row)
        name, kind, path, sha256, md5 = row
        assert name not in seen_modules
        seen_modules.add(name)
        assert len(md5) == 32 and all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in md5)
        if kind == 'preloaded':
            assert path == sha256 == ''
        elif kind == 'disk':
            assert path.startswith((str(E) + '/', str(R) + '/'))
            assert path in before['files'] and before['files'][path][9] == sha256
        elif kind == 'project':
            expected = next(item for item in projects if item['module'] == name)
            assert (path, sha256) == (expected['file'], expected['sha256'])
            assert next(item for item in frame['projects'] if item['module'] == name)['loaded_md5'] == md5
        else:
            raise AssertionError('unproved module origin')
    assert all(row['module'] in seen_modules for row in projects)
    # Only complete actual typed/source/core/project/lock/origin proof establishes used.
    child_used_proof = frame['owner']['used']
    assert child_used_proof <= 67108864 - owner_work_bytes
    owner_work_bytes += child_used_proof
    child_debited = child_used_proof
except BaseException as error:
    failures.append('candidate:' + type(error).__name__)
    if isinstance(error, BaseExceptionGroup):
        failures.extend('candidate-component:' + type(item).__name__ for item in error.exceptions[:8])
finally:
    if runner_attempted and child_used_proof is None:
        remaining_hold = child_allowance - child_debited
        assert 0 <= remaining_hold <= 67108864 - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += remaining_hold
        child_debited += remaining_hold
    if result is None:
        result = {'pid': None, 'exit': None, 'failures': [], 'group_gone': not runner_attempted,
                  'output_encoding': 'hex', 'stdout': '', 'stderr': ''}
    failures.extend(result['failures'])

tree_start = owner_work_bytes
tree_ceiling = min(67108864, tree_start + tree_reserve)
try:
    assert result['group_gone'], 'live or unproved group retains stage'
    if stage_acquired:
        assert stage_identity is not None
        parent = stage.parent.lstat()
        assert (parent.st_dev, parent.st_ino, parent.st_mode, parent.st_uid) == (
            temporary_parent_info.st_dev, temporary_parent_info.st_ino,
            temporary_parent_info.st_mode, temporary_parent_info.st_uid)
        assert 131072 <= tree_ceiling - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += 131072
        pending, contents, tree_bytes, inspected_bytes = [stage], {}, 2, 0
        directory_identities = set()
        while pending:
            directory = pending.pop()
            relative = str(directory.relative_to(stage))
            relative = '' if relative == '.' else relative
            assert relative in allowed_directories
            assert len(directory.parts) - len(stage.parts) <= 16
            assert len(str(directory).encode('utf-8')) <= 4096 and len(tree) < 256
            info = directory.lstat()
            assert stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) and stat.S_IMODE(info.st_mode) == 0o700
            assert info.st_uid == os.getuid() and (info.st_dev, info.st_ino) not in directory_identities
            if directory == stage:
                assert (info.st_dev, info.st_ino) == stage_identity
            charge = 2048 + 16 * len(str(directory).encode('utf-8'))
            assert charge <= tree_ceiling - owner_work_bytes
            owner_work_bytes += charge
            directory_identities.add((info.st_dev, info.st_ino))
            tree[relative] = ['directory', info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_mode, info.st_uid,
                              info.st_nlink, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns]
            names = os.listdir(directory)
            assert len(names) <= 256 and len(tree) + len(names) <= 256
            assert 1024 + 256 * len(names) <= tree_ceiling - owner_work_bytes
            owner_work_bytes += 1024 + 256 * len(names)
            for name in sorted(names):
                assert 0 < len(name.encode('utf-8', 'strict')) <= 255
                assert name not in ('.', '..') and '/' not in name and '\0' not in name
                path = directory / name
                key = path.relative_to(stage).as_posix()
                assert len(str(path).encode('utf-8')) <= 4096 and len(tree) < 256
                charge = 2048 + 16 * (len(name.encode('utf-8')) + len(str(path).encode('utf-8')))
                assert charge <= tree_ceiling - owner_work_bytes
                owner_work_bytes += charge
                entry = path.lstat()
                assert entry.st_uid == os.getuid() and not stat.S_ISLNK(entry.st_mode)
                if stat.S_ISDIR(entry.st_mode):
                    assert key in allowed_directories
                    assert len(path.parts) - len(stage.parts) <= 16
                    pending.append(path)
                    continue
                assert stat.S_ISREG(entry.st_mode) and 0 <= entry.st_size <= 1048576
                if path.parent == pub_directory:
                    assert name.startswith('port_') and name[5:].isascii() and name[5:].isdigit()
                    port = int(name[5:])
                    assert 1 <= port <= 65535 and name == 'port_' + str(port)
                    assert entry.st_size == 0 and entry.st_nlink == 1
                elif path.parent == lock_directory:
                    assert name == 'lock_0' or name.startswith('port_')
                    if name != 'lock_0':
                        assert name[5:].isascii() and name[5:].isdigit()
                        port = int(name[5:])
                        assert 1 <= port <= 65535 and name == 'port_' + str(port)
                    assert entry.st_size == 75 and entry.st_nlink == 2
                elif path.parent == stage / 'tmp' and name.startswith('mix_user_check_'):
                    parts = name[len('mix_user_check_'):].split('_')
                    assert len(parts) == 2 and parts[0].isascii() and parts[0].isdigit()
                    assert len(parts[1]) == 4 and all(c in alphabet for c in parts[1])
                    assert entry.st_size == 0 and entry.st_nlink == 1 and frame is None
                else:
                    raise AssertionError('unknown owned-tree entry; retain stage')
                assert entry.st_size <= 8388608 - inspected_bytes
                assert entry.st_size <= 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes
                fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
                read_error = None
                try:
                    opened = os.fstat(fd)
                    assert tuple(getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
                        getattr(entry, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
                    size, digest, data = 0, hashlib.sha256(), bytearray()
                    while size < opened.st_size:
                        count = min(65536, opened.st_size - size, 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes,
                                    8388608 - inspected_bytes)
                        assert count > 0
                        chunk = os.read(fd, count)
                        snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
                        inspected_bytes += len(chunk)
                        size += len(chunk)
                        assert chunk, 'short EOF reading owned tree'
                        digest.update(chunk)
                        if entry.st_size == 75:
                            data.extend(chunk)
                    stable = os.fstat(fd)
                    assert tuple(getattr(stable, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
                        getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
                except BaseException as error:
                    read_error = error
                finally:
                    try:
                        os.close(fd)
                    except BaseException as close_error:
                        if read_error is not None:
                            raise BaseExceptionGroup('tree read and close', [read_error, close_error])
                        raise
                if read_error is not None:
                    raise read_error
                current = path.lstat()
                assert tuple(getattr(current, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
                    getattr(entry, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
                row = ['regular', entry.st_dev, entry.st_ino, entry.st_mode, entry.st_uid,
                       entry.st_nlink, size, entry.st_mtime_ns, entry.st_ctime_ns, digest.hexdigest()]
                fragment = json.dumps({key: row}, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
                tree_bytes += len(fragment) + 1
                assert tree_bytes <= 262144
                tree[key] = row
                if data:
                    contents[key] = bytes(data)
            stable = directory.lstat()
            assert tuple(getattr(stable, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
                getattr(info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
        assert 2 * 262144 + 4096 + 512 * len(tree) <= tree_ceiling - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += 2 * 262144 + 4096 + 512 * len(tree)
        assert len(json.dumps(tree, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')) <= 262144
        lock_rows = sorted(key for key in tree if str(stage / key).startswith(str(lock_directory) + '/'))
        pub_rows = [key for key in tree if str(stage / key).startswith(str(pub_directory) + '/')]
        assert len(pub_rows) <= 1 and len(lock_rows) in (0, 2)
        if frame is not None:
            assert lock_rows == sorted([str((lock_directory / 'lock_0').relative_to(stage)),
                                       str((lock_directory / ('port_' + str(frame['lock']['port']))).relative_to(stage))])
            assert not pub_rows or str(stage / pub_rows[0]) == frame['pubsub']['path']
        if lock_rows:
            left, right = lock_rows
            assert tree[left][1:6] == tree[right][1:6] and tree[left][5] == 2
            assert contents[left] == contents[right]
            bytes_value = contents[left]
            assert len(bytes_value) == 75 and bytes_value[0] in (0, 1)
            segments = (bytes_value[1:38], bytes_value[38:75])
            assert segments[bytes_value[0]] == bytes(4) + bytes([32]) + bytes(32)
            port_name = next(pathlib.Path(key).name for key in lock_rows if pathlib.Path(key).name != 'lock_0')
            port = int(port_name[5:])
            pid_bytes = str(result['pid']).encode('ascii')
            assert result['pid'] is not None and 0 < len(pid_bytes) <= 32
            expected = port.to_bytes(4, 'big') + bytes([32 - len(pid_bytes)]) + bytes(32 - len(pid_bytes)) + pid_bytes
            assert segments[1 - bytes_value[0]] == expected
            if frame is not None:
                assert bytes_value.hex() == frame['lock']['released']
        survivor_ctime = None
        regular_keys = sorted(key for key, row in tree.items() if row[0] == 'regular')
        for key in regular_keys:
            path, row = stage / key, tree[key]
            current = path.lstat()
            expected_links = 1 if key in lock_rows and any(item in removals for item in lock_rows) else row[5]
            assert (current.st_dev, current.st_ino, current.st_mode, current.st_uid, current.st_nlink,
                    current.st_size, current.st_mtime_ns) == (row[1], row[2], row[3], row[4], expected_links, row[6], row[7])
            assert current.st_ctime_ns == (row[8] if expected_links == row[5] else survivor_ctime)
            os.unlink(path)
            assert not os.path.lexists(path)
            removals.append(key)
            if key in lock_rows:
                survivors = [item for item in lock_rows if item not in removals]
                if survivors:
                    other, other_row = stage / survivors[0], tree[survivors[0]]
                    survivor = other.lstat()
                    survivor_ctime = survivor.st_ctime_ns
                    assert (survivor.st_dev, survivor.st_ino, survivor.st_mode, survivor.st_uid,
                            survivor.st_nlink, survivor.st_size, survivor.st_mtime_ns) == (
                        other_row[1], other_row[2], other_row[3], other_row[4], 1, other_row[6], other_row[7])
        directory_keys = sorted((key for key, row in tree.items() if row[0] == 'directory'),
                                key=lambda value: (value.count('/'), len(value)), reverse=True)
        for key in directory_keys:
            path, row = stage / key, tree[key]
            current = path.lstat()
            assert (current.st_dev, current.st_ino, current.st_mode, current.st_uid) == (row[1], row[2], row[3], row[4])
            assert not os.listdir(path)
            os.rmdir(path)
            assert not os.path.lexists(path)
            removals.append(key)
        assert not os.path.lexists(stage)
        stage_clean = True
    else:
        stage_clean = not os.path.lexists(stage)
except BaseException as error:
    failures.append('stage:' + type(error).__name__)
    if isinstance(error, BaseExceptionGroup):
        failures.extend('stage-component:' + type(item).__name__ for item in error.exceptions[:8])
assert owner_work_bytes - tree_start <= tree_reserve
after_start = owner_work_bytes
try:
    owner_work_limit = min(67108864, after_start + after_reserve)
    after = snapshot_preflight_inputs()
    unchanged = after == before
    assert unchanged
except BaseException as error:
    failures.append('after:' + type(error).__name__)
    if isinstance(error, BaseExceptionGroup):
        failures.extend('after-component:' + type(item).__name__ for item in error.exceptions[:8])
finally:
    owner_work_limit = 67108864
assert owner_work_bytes - after_start <= after_reserve

conditional_ready = (
    not failures and child_used_proof is not None and result['exit'] == 0
    and result['group_gone'] and unchanged and stage_clean and frame is not None
)
try:
    assert pre_archived and trace_identity is not None
    assert len(failures) <= 64 and all(type(value) is str and len(value) <= 128 for value in failures)
    post_value = {
        'kind': 'R-MIX1 actual conditional project result; external post Git receipt still required',
        'result': result, 'frame': frame, 'after': after, 'stage_tree': tree, 'removals': removals,
        'failures': failures, 'input_read_bytes_before_final_readback': snapshot_read_bytes,
        'owner_work_bytes': owner_work_bytes, 'child_debited': child_debited,
        'child_used_proof': child_used_proof, 'stage_gone': stage_clean,
        'inventory_unchanged': unchanged, 'conditional_ready_before_archive_close': conditional_ready,
        'external_pre_and_post_git_required': True, 'program_observed_git': False,
    }
    post_size = 1
    for fragment in json.JSONEncoder(sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).iterencode(post_value):
        post_size += len(fragment.encode('ascii'))
        assert post_size <= post_reserve and len(pre_record) + post_size <= 2097152
    post_record = (json.dumps(post_value, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')) + '\n').encode('ascii')
    assert len(post_record) == post_size
    log_info = LOG.lstat()
    assert stat.S_ISREG(log_info.st_mode) and log_info.st_nlink == 1
    assert (log_info.st_dev, log_info.st_ino) == trace_identity
    assert (log_info.st_mode, log_info.st_uid, log_info.st_size, log_info.st_mtime_ns, log_info.st_ctime_ns) == (
        written.st_mode, written.st_uid, written.st_size, written.st_mtime_ns, written.st_ctime_ns)
    assert log_info.st_size + post_size <= 8388608
    assert log_info.st_size <= 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes
    fd = os.open(LOG, os.O_RDWR | os.O_APPEND | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
    archive_error = None
    try:
        opened = os.fstat(fd)
        assert tuple(getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
            getattr(log_info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
        for part in (trace_prefix, pre_record):
            offset = 0
            while offset < len(part):
                count = min(65536, len(part) - offset, 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes)
                assert count > 0
                chunk = os.read(fd, count)
                snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
                assert chunk and chunk == part[offset:offset + len(chunk)]
                offset += len(chunk)
        stable = os.fstat(fd)
        assert tuple(getattr(stable, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
            getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
        offset = 0
        while offset < len(post_record):
            count = os.write(fd, post_record[offset:offset + 65536])
            assert count > 0
            offset += count
        os.fsync(fd)
        written_post = os.fstat(fd)
        assert (written_post.st_dev, written_post.st_ino, written_post.st_mode,
                written_post.st_uid, written_post.st_nlink) == (
            opened.st_dev, opened.st_ino, opened.st_mode, opened.st_uid, opened.st_nlink)
        assert written_post.st_size == opened.st_size + len(post_record)
        assert written_post.st_size <= 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes
        os.lseek(fd, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
        for part in (trace_prefix, pre_record, post_record):
            offset = 0
            while offset < len(part):
                count = min(65536, len(part) - offset, 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes)
                assert count > 0
                chunk = os.read(fd, count)
                snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
                assert chunk and chunk == part[offset:offset + len(chunk)]
                offset += len(chunk)
        stable = os.fstat(fd)
        assert tuple(getattr(stable, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
            getattr(written_post, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
    except BaseException as error:
        archive_error = error
    finally:
        try:
            os.close(fd)
        except BaseException as close_error:
            if archive_error is not None:
                raise BaseExceptionGroup('postarchive and close', [archive_error, close_error])
            raise
    if archive_error is not None:
        raise archive_error
    current = LOG.lstat()
    assert tuple(getattr(current, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
        getattr(written_post, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
except BaseException as error:
    conditional_ready = False
    failures.append('postarchive:' + type(error).__name__)
    if isinstance(error, BaseExceptionGroup):
        failures.extend('postarchive-component:' + type(item).__name__ for item in error.exceptions[:8])
summary = {
    'pid': result['pid'], 'exit': result['exit'], 'group_gone': result['group_gone'],
    'input_read_bytes': snapshot_read_bytes, 'owner_work_bytes': owner_work_bytes,
    'stage_gone': stage_clean, 'inventory_unchanged': unchanged,
    'conditional_project_ready': conditional_ready, 'fresh_root_pre_post_git_still_required': True,
    'failures': failures,
}
summary_bytes = (json.dumps(summary, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')) + '\n').encode('ascii')
assert len(summary_bytes) <= 16384
offset = 0
while offset < len(summary_bytes):
    count = os.write(1, summary_bytes[offset:])
    assert count > 0
    offset += count
if not conditional_ready:
    raise SystemExit(1)
```

def snapshot_preflight_inputs():
    global owner_work_bytes, snapshot_read_bytes, owner_work_limit
    assert 131072 <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
    owner_work_bytes += 131072
    # One bounded two-chunk peak; chunks are hashed immediately and never retained in rows.
    files, directories, raw_members = {}, {}, {}
    paths, seen_descendants = set(PINNED), set()
    metadata_bytes, entry_count, directory_count = 128, 0, 0
    raw_name_bytes, joined_path_bytes, maximum_depth, match_count = 0, 0, 0, 0
    matches = {'guides': [], 'docs': []}
    paths.add(str(NOTE))
    for root in (ROOT, E, R, B):
        for parent in (root, *root.parents):
            text = str(parent)
            assert len(text.encode('utf-8', 'strict')) <= 4096
            info = parent.lstat()
            assert stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) and not stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode)
            header = ['directory', info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_mode, info.st_uid,
                      info.st_nlink, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns]
            if text in directories:
                assert directories[text] == header
                continue
            assert len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) < 4096
            charge = 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
            assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
            owner_work_bytes += charge
            fragment = json.dumps({text: header}, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
            metadata_bytes += len(fragment) + 1
            assert metadata_bytes <= 524288
            directories[text] = header
    for absent in (pathlib.Path('/Users/yoavgea/.erlang'), ROOT / '.erlang',
                   B / '.erlang', B / 'erl_crash.dump'):
        assert not os.path.lexists(absent)
        text = str(absent)
        assert len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) < 4096
        assert 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8')) <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
        raw_members[text] = None
        metadata_bytes += 8 + 6 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
        assert metadata_bytes <= 524288
    for root in (E, R):
        for pattern in ('lib/**/ebin/*.beam', 'lib/**/ebin/*.app', 'lib/**/priv/lib/*.so'):
            for path in root.glob(pattern):
                text = str(path)
                assert len(text.encode('utf-8', 'strict')) <= 4096
                if text not in paths:
                    assert len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) < 4096
                    charge = 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
                    assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
                    owner_work_bytes += charge
                    paths.add(text)
    for path in (E / 'lib/elixir/lib/system.ex', E / 'lib/elixir/lib/json.ex',
                 R / 'releases/RELEASES', R / 'releases/29/installed_application_versions',
                 R / 'bin/start.boot', R / 'releases/29/start_clean.script'):
        text = str(path)
        assert text in paths or len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) < 4096
        assert 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8')) <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
        paths.add(text)
    pending = [(B, -1), (B / 'guides', 0), (B / 'docs', 0)]
    while pending:
        directory, depth = pending.pop()
        text = str(directory)
        assert depth <= 16 and len(text.encode('utf-8', 'strict')) <= 4096
        if not os.path.lexists(directory):
            assert directory in (B / 'guides', B / 'docs')
            assert 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8')) <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
            owner_work_bytes += 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
            raw_members[text] = None
            metadata_bytes += 8 + 6 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
            assert metadata_bytes <= 524288
            continue
        info = directory.lstat()
        assert stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) and not stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode)
        identity = (info.st_dev, info.st_ino)
        assert identity not in seen_descendants
        assert directory_count < 128
        directory_count += 1
        maximum_depth = max(maximum_depth, max(depth, 0))
        assert len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) < 4096
        assert 4096 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8')) <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += 4096 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
        seen_descendants.add(identity)
        header = ['directory', info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_mode, info.st_uid,
                  info.st_nlink, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns]
        if text in directories:
            assert directories[text] == header
        else:
            directories[text] = header
            metadata_bytes += 256 + 6 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
        # Native listdir returns names before admission; this is not a native heap quota.
        names = os.listdir(directory)
        assert len(names) <= 256 and entry_count + len(names) <= 1024
        charge = 1024 + 256 * len(names)
        assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += charge
        names = sorted(names)
        rows = []
        for name in names:
            name_bytes = name.encode('utf-8', 'strict')
            assert name not in ('', '.', '..') and '/' not in name and '\0' not in name
            assert len(name_bytes) <= 255 and entry_count < 1024
            entry = directory / name
            value = str(entry)
            value_bytes = value.encode('utf-8', 'strict')
            assert len(value_bytes) <= 4096
            assert raw_name_bytes + len(name_bytes) <= 131072
            assert joined_path_bytes + len(value_bytes) <= 262144
            assert len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) + entry_count < 4096
            entry_count += 1
            raw_name_bytes += len(name_bytes)
            joined_path_bytes += len(value_bytes)
            charge = 2048 + 16 * (len(name_bytes) + len(value_bytes))
            assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
            owner_work_bytes += charge
            entry_info = entry.lstat()
            assert stat.S_ISREG(entry_info.st_mode) or stat.S_ISDIR(entry_info.st_mode)
            assert not stat.S_ISLNK(entry_info.st_mode)
            if stat.S_ISREG(entry_info.st_mode):
                assert entry_info.st_nlink == 1
            kind = 'directory' if stat.S_ISDIR(entry_info.st_mode) else 'regular'
            entry_header = [kind, entry_info.st_dev, entry_info.st_ino, entry_info.st_mode,
                            entry_info.st_uid, entry_info.st_nlink, entry_info.st_size,
                            entry_info.st_mtime_ns, entry_info.st_ctime_ns]
            fragment = json.dumps([name, entry_header], separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
            metadata_bytes += len(fragment) + 1
            assert metadata_bytes <= 524288
            rows.append([name, entry_header])
            if depth >= 0 and kind == 'directory':
                assert depth < 16
                pending.append((entry, depth + 1))
            if depth >= 0 and name.endswith('.md'):
                relative = entry.relative_to(B)
                visible = all(not part.startswith('.') for part in relative.parts)
                eligible = relative.parts[0] == 'docs' or depth == 0
                if visible and eligible:
                    key = relative.parts[0]
                    rendered = '../../' + relative.as_posix()
                    charge = 1024 + 16 * len(rendered.encode('utf-8'))
                    assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
                    owner_work_bytes += charge
                    matches[key].append(rendered)
                    match_count += 1
        raw_members[text] = rows
        final = directory.lstat()
        assert (final.st_dev, final.st_ino, final.st_mode, final.st_uid, final.st_nlink,
                final.st_size, final.st_mtime_ns, final.st_ctime_ns) == tuple(header[1:])
    for key in ('guides', 'docs'):
        assert 1024 + 256 * len(matches[key]) <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += 1024 + 256 * len(matches[key])
        matches[key] = sorted(matches[key])
    assert len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) + entry_count <= 4096
    charge = 2048 + 128 * len(paths)
    assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
    owner_work_bytes += charge
    for text in sorted(paths):
        assert len(text.encode('utf-8', 'strict')) <= 4096
        path = pathlib.Path(text)
        for parent in path.parents:
            value = str(parent)
            info = parent.lstat()
            assert stat.S_ISDIR(info.st_mode) and not stat.S_ISLNK(info.st_mode)
            header = ['directory', info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_mode, info.st_uid,
                      info.st_nlink, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns]
            if value in directories:
                assert directories[value] == header
            else:
                assert len(paths) + len(directories) + len(raw_members) + entry_count < 4096
                charge = 1024 + 16 * len(value.encode('utf-8', 'strict'))
                assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
                owner_work_bytes += charge
                fragment = json.dumps({value: header}, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
                metadata_bytes += len(fragment) + 1
                assert metadata_bytes <= 524288
                directories[value] = header
        info = path.lstat()
        assert stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) and info.st_nlink == 1
        assert 0 <= info.st_size <= 33554432
        assert info.st_size <= 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes
        charge = 1024 + 16 * len(text.encode('utf-8'))
        assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
        owner_work_bytes += charge
        read_error = None
        fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
        try:
            opened = os.fstat(fd)
            assert tuple(getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
                getattr(info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
            size, digest = 0, hashlib.sha256()
            while size < info.st_size:
                count = min(65536, info.st_size - size, 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes)
                assert count > 0
                chunk = os.read(fd, count)
                snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
                size += len(chunk)
                assert chunk, 'short EOF before admitted input size'
                digest.update(chunk)
            final = os.fstat(fd)
            assert tuple(getattr(final, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
                getattr(info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
        except BaseException as error:
            read_error = error
        finally:
            try:
                os.close(fd)
            except BaseException as close_error:
                if read_error is not None:
                    raise BaseExceptionGroup('input read and close', [read_error, close_error])
                raise
        if read_error is not None:
            raise read_error
        current = path.lstat()
        assert tuple(getattr(current, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
            getattr(info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
        sha256 = digest.hexdigest()
        if text in PINNED:
            assert sha256 == PINNED[text]
        row = ['regular', info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_mode, info.st_uid,
               info.st_nlink, size, info.st_mtime_ns, info.st_ctime_ns, sha256]
        fragment = json.dumps({text: row}, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
        metadata_bytes += len(fragment) + 1
        assert metadata_bytes <= 524288
        files[text] = row
    census = {
        'entries': entry_count, 'directories': directory_count, 'raw_name_bytes': raw_name_bytes,
        'joined_path_bytes': joined_path_bytes, 'maximum_depth': maximum_depth, 'matches': match_count,
    }
    charge = 3 * 524288 + 2048 + 256 * (len(files) + len(directories) + len(raw_members))
    assert charge <= owner_work_limit - owner_work_bytes
    owner_work_bytes += charge
    snapshot = {
        'files': files, 'directories': directories, 'raw_members': raw_members,
        'matches': matches, 'census': census,
    }
    encoded = json.dumps(snapshot, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':')).encode('ascii')
    assert len(encoded) <= metadata_bytes <= 524288
    return snapshot

def run_cached_preflight(argv, environment):
    global owner_work_bytes, frame, runner_attempted
    assert 532480 <= 67108864 - owner_work_bytes
    owner_work_bytes += 532480
    assert owner_work_bytes == boot['parent_work']
    buffers = {'stdout': bytearray(), 'stderr': bytearray()}
    process, selector, gone = None, None, False
    causes = []
    frame = None
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 30
    try:
        runner_attempted = True
        process = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=B, env=environment, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, start_new_session=True)
        assert time.monotonic() < deadline
        selector = selectors.DefaultSelector()
        for name, stream in (('stdout', process.stdout), ('stderr', process.stderr)):
            os.set_blocking(stream.fileno(), False)
            selector.register(stream, selectors.EVENT_READ, name)
        while selector.get_map() or process.poll() is None:
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            assert remaining > 0, 'VM supervision30second deadline'
            for key, _ in selector.select(min(0.1, remaining)):
                chunk = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 4096)
                if not chunk:
                    selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
                    key.fileobj.close()
                    continue
                assert len(chunk) <= 65536 - len(buffers[key.data])
                buffers[key.data].extend(chunk)
        process.wait(timeout=max(0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    except BaseException as error:
        causes.append('runner:' + type(error).__name__)
    finally:
        if process is None:
            gone = not runner_attempted
        else:
            try:
                gone = terminate_preflight_group(process)
            except BaseException as error:
                causes.append('group:' + type(error).__name__)
        if selector is not None:
            try:
                selector.close()
            except BaseException as error:
                causes.append('selector:' + type(error).__name__)
        if process is not None:
            for stream in (process.stdout, process.stderr):
                if stream is not None and not stream.closed:
                    try:
                        stream.close()
                    except BaseException as error:
                        causes.append('pipe:' + type(error).__name__)
    result = {
        'pid': None if process is None else process.pid,
        'exit': None if process is None else process.returncode, 'failures': causes,
        'group_gone': gone, 'output_encoding': 'hex',
        'stdout': bytes(buffers['stdout']).hex(), 'stderr': bytes(buffers['stderr']).hex(),
    }
    try:
        assert result['exit'] == 0 and not causes and gone
        stdout, stderr = bytes(buffers['stdout']), bytes(buffers['stderr'])
        prefix = b'WF_SUPPORTED_PROJECT_READINESS_V1 '
        assert stderr == b'' and len(stdout) <= 32768
        assert stdout.startswith(prefix) and stdout.endswith(b'\n') and stdout.count(b'\n') == 1
        frame = json.loads(stdout[len(prefix):-1].decode('utf-8', 'strict'),
                           object_pairs_hook=unique_json_object)
        assert type(frame) is dict and set(frame) == FRAME_KEYS
        assert type(frame['schema_version']) is int and frame['schema_version'] == 1
        assert frame['kind'] == 'supported-original-project-readiness'
        use = frame['owner']
        assert type(use) is dict and set(use) == {'parent_work', 'future_reserve', 'child_allowance', 'used'}
        assert all(type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= 67108864 for value in use.values())
        assert (use['parent_work'], use['future_reserve'], use['child_allowance']) == (
            boot['parent_work'], future_reserve, child_allowance)
        assert use['used'] <= child_allowance
        core = frame['core']
        assert type(core) is dict and set(core) == CORE_KEYS
        assert (core['pid'], core['elixir_version'], core['otp_release'], core['erts_version'], core['code_root']) == (
            str(result['pid']), '1.20.4', '29', '17.0.5', str(R))
        build = core['build_info']
        assert type(build) is dict and set(build) == BUILD_INFO_KEYS
        assert all(type(value) is str and len(value.encode('utf-8')) <= 4096 for value in build.values())
        assert build['version'] == '1.20.4' and build['otp_release'] == '29'
        assert type(core['architecture']) is str and len(core['architecture'].encode('utf-8')) <= 4096
        assert core['architecture'].startswith(('aarch64-apple-darwin', 'arm64-apple-darwin'))
        assert core['os_type'] == ['unix', 'darwin']
        assert type(core['os_version']) is list and len(core['os_version']) == 3
        assert all(type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= 18446744073709551615 for value in core['os_version'])
        assert core['os_version'] == [int(value) for value in os.uname().release.split('.')]
        assert type(core['modules']) is list and len(core['modules']) == 4
        for expected, actual in zip(modules, core['modules']):
            assert type(actual) is dict and set(actual) == {'module', 'path', 'sha256', 'loaded_md5'}
            assert all(type(value) is str for value in actual.values())
            assert {key: actual[key] for key in expected} == expected
            assert len(actual['loaded_md5']) == 32 and all(c in '0123456789abcdef' for c in actual['loaded_md5'])
        assert core['sha256_abc'] == hashlib.sha256(b'abc').hexdigest()
        assert type(core['json_encoded']) is str and len(core['json_encoded'].encode('utf-8')) <= 1024
        for decoded in (json.loads(core['json_encoded'], object_pairs_hook=unique_json_object), core['json_decoded']):
            assert type(decoded) is dict and set(decoded) == {'empty', 'text', 'values'}
            assert type(decoded['empty']) is list and decoded['empty'] == []
            assert type(decoded['text']) is str and decoded['text'] == 'é\n"\\'
            assert type(decoded['values']) is list and len(decoded['values']) == 5
            assert [type(value) for value in decoded['values']] == [type(None), bool, bool, int, int]
            assert decoded['values'] == [None, True, False, 0, 18446744073709551615]
        assert type(core['crypto_libraries']) is list and 1 <= len(core['crypto_libraries']) <= 4
        for row in core['crypto_libraries']:
            assert type(row) is dict and set(row) == {'name', 'version', 'text'}
            assert all(type(row[key]) is str and len(row[key].encode('utf-8')) <= 256 for key in ('name', 'text'))
            assert type(row['version']) is int and 0 <= row['version'] <= 18446744073709551615
    except BaseException as error:
        frame = None
        causes.append('core-frame:' + type(error).__name__)
    return result

def terminate_preflight_group(process):
    term_end = time.monotonic() + 2
    kill_end = term_end + 2
    try:
        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        process.wait(timeout=max(0, term_end - time.monotonic()))
        return True
    while time.monotonic() < term_end:
        process.poll()
        try:
            os.killpg(process.pid, 0)
        except ProcessLookupError:
            process.wait(timeout=max(0, term_end - time.monotonic()))
            return True
        time.sleep(min(0.02, max(0, term_end - time.monotonic())))
    try:
        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        pass
    while time.monotonic() < kill_end:
        process.poll()
        try:
            os.killpg(process.pid, 0)
        except ProcessLookupError:
            process.wait(timeout=max(0, kill_end - time.monotonic()))
            return True
        time.sleep(min(0.02, max(0, kill_end - time.monotonic())))
    return False

def unique_json_object(pairs):
    result = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ValueError('duplicate JSON object key')
        result[key] = value
    return result

  lock_path = Path.join([boot["stage"], "tmp", "mix_lock_user#{uid}", key])

assert os.uname().sysname == 'Darwin' and os.uname().machine == 'arm64'
assert os.environ.get('HOME') == '/Users/yoavgea'
for parent in NOTE.parents:
    assert stat.S_ISDIR(parent.lstat().st_mode) and not parent.is_symlink()
carrier_info = NOTE.lstat()
assert stat.S_ISREG(carrier_info.st_mode) and carrier_info.st_nlink == 1
assert 0 <= carrier_info.st_size <= 524288 and carrier_info.st_size <= 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes
assert 4 * carrier_info.st_size + 8192 <= 67108864 - owner_work_bytes
owner_work_bytes += 4 * carrier_info.st_size + 8192
carrier_error = None
fd = os.open(NOTE, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
try:
    opened = os.fstat(fd)
    assert tuple(getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
        getattr(carrier_info, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
    carrier_buffer = bytearray()
    while len(carrier_buffer) < opened.st_size:
        count = min(65536, opened.st_size - len(carrier_buffer), 268435456 - snapshot_read_bytes)
        assert count > 0
        chunk = os.read(fd, count)
        snapshot_read_bytes += len(chunk)
        assert chunk, 'short EOF before admitted carrier size'
        carrier_buffer.extend(chunk)
    final = os.fstat(fd)
    assert tuple(getattr(final, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS) == tuple(
        getattr(opened, field) for field in STAT_FIELDS)
except BaseException as error:
    carrier_error = error
finally:
    try:
        os.close(fd)
    except BaseException as close_error:
        if carrier_error is not None:
            raise BaseExceptionGroup('carrier read and close', [carrier_error, close_error])
        raise
if carrier_error is not None:
    raise carrier_error

    stage_info = stage.lstat()
    assert stat.S_ISDIR(stage_info.st_mode) and stat.S_IMODE(stage_info.st_mode) == 0o700
    assert stage_info.st_uid == os.getuid()
    stage_identity = (stage_info.st_dev, stage_info.st_ino)
    for relative in (
        'tmp', 'mix-home', 'mix-home/archives', 'build', 'build/test',
        'tmp/mix_pubsub_user' + str(os.getuid()), str(pub_directory.relative_to(stage)),
        'tmp/mix_lock_user' + str(os.getuid()), str(lock_directory.relative_to(stage)),
    ):
        directory = stage / relative
        assert relative in allowed_directories and len(str(directory).encode('utf-8')) <= 4096
        immediate_parent = directory.parent.lstat()
        for parent in (directory.parent, *directory.parent.parents):
            if parent == stage.parent:
                break
            parent_info = parent.lstat()
            parent_relative = parent.relative_to(stage).as_posix()
            assert ('' if parent_relative == '.' else parent_relative) in allowed_directories
            assert stat.S_ISDIR(parent_info.st_mode) and not stat.S_ISLNK(parent_info.st_mode)
            assert stat.S_IMODE(parent_info.st_mode) == 0o700 and parent_info.st_uid == os.getuid()
            if parent == stage:
                assert (parent_info.st_dev, parent_info.st_ino) == stage_identity
        os.mkdir(directory, 0o700)
        created = directory.lstat()
        assert stat.S_ISDIR(created.st_mode) and not stat.S_ISLNK(created.st_mode)
        assert stat.S_IMODE(created.st_mode) == 0o700 and created.st_uid == os.getuid()
        current_parent = directory.parent.lstat()
        assert (current_parent.st_dev, current_parent.st_ino, current_parent.st_mode, current_parent.st_uid) == (
            immediate_parent.st_dev, immediate_parent.st_ino, immediate_parent.st_mode, immediate_parent.st_uid)
